What first generates and gathers can also name the receiving destination, allowing the abyss to function as an inverted maternal center.","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"macro","source_local_id":"A:Blow, Descent, and Dispersive Impact","source_type":"channel","support_id":"sup_5b99d618fdc40d0dae53","text":"A blow or downward cast strikes a target and sends what was gathered into scattered motion.","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":true,"role":"context_only","scope":"macro","source_local_id":"A:Leader, Exemplar, and Beacon","source_type":"channel","support_id":"sup_5d18139d6e1b9f9ed13a","text":"101:1-3 القارعة (ق ر ع); 101:5 الجبال (ج ب ل); 101:9 أمّه (ء م م); 101:11 نار (ن و ر)","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":true,"role":"context_only","scope":"macro","source_local_id":"D:Bared Surface and Empty Interior","source_type":"channel","support_id":"sup_62a40b3ad8118c32cf7e","text":"Bodies and places are organized by outer surfaces, supporting layers, bounded openings, and the empty spaces those boundaries contain.","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"macro","source_local_id":"A:Blow, Descent, and Dispersive Impact","source_type":"channel","support_id":"sup_62dd3c32e5699887e8ef","text":"The قارعة is read through the concrete action of one thing striking another. The hand or weapon descends toward its object, and the impact converts cohesion into broadcast fragments or bodies.","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000053/B014","root_000427/B007","root_001332/B004"],"citable":true,"role":"branch_nomination","scope":"macro","source_local_id":"D:Submission and Bonded Service","source_type":"channel","support_id":"sup_637d82dcc1c733b72ab8","text":"submission or abasement (ك و ن:B004/m01); obedience and compliance (خ ف ف:B007/m01); bondmaid or young female servant (ء م م:B014/m01); obedient relation (ر ض ي:B004/m01)","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"macro","source_local_id":"A:Human Community and Multitude","source_type":"channel","support_id":"sup_63a04b8842982174a5a3","text":"Individual humans become a named community whose scale ranges from a small group to a mountain-like multitude.","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"macro","source_local_id":"A:Bounded Day and Extended Interval","source_type":"channel","support_id":"sup_64e1682f33bd81dca6c4","text":"The ordinary day anchors several scales of duration. Its midpoint is imagined as a poised balance, while its lexical field can widen to an age, a delay, or an extended passage through night.","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":true,"role":"context_only","scope":"macro","source_local_id":"B:Guide-Line and Constructed Alignment","source_type":"channel","support_id":"sup_67bbb87c6a14ccbb8143","text":"A selected center or standard organizes direction, alignment, imitation, destination, and responsible action.","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"macro","source_local_id":"B:Cranial Breach and Gaping Wound","source_type":"channel","support_id":"sup_68362cbbbedb303d36ed","text":"The striking action becomes a bodily penetration scene: force breaks through the head, reaches the thin supporting layer, and exposes the deeper cranial center as a gaping interior.","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":true,"role":"context_only","scope":"macro","source_local_id":"B:Cranial Breach and Gaping Wound","source_type":"channel","support_id":"sup_6b5283c6d52fb71c3743","text":"Directed force meets a resistant surface and produces displacement, penetration, fracture, or an opened interior.","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"macro","source_local_id":"A:Mother as Origin and Destination","source_type":"channel","support_id":"sup_6e32120fefa1586025fb","text":"A maternal source gathers dependents and also names the place to which one returns or is delivered.","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000427/B008","root_001143/B004","root_001534/B003","root_001609/B006"],"citable":true,"role":"branch_nomination","scope":"macro","source_local_id":"C:Night-Roaming Herd","source_type":"channel","support_id":"sup_6f877cf55b4f221a75fa","text":"livestock dispersing at night without a shepherd (ن ف ش:B003/m01);
